- **openspec-reverse-update-spec**: 根据代码变更反向更新 OpenSpec 四个工件（proposal/design/specs/tasks）。当用户说"反向更新spec"、"反向更新工件"、"我自己修改了代码，更新下spec文档"时使用此技能。支持无输入（分析 Git 变更）或指定文件路径。输出更新建议后必须询问用户确认再执行更新。 ([kinneyyan/prompts](https://github.com/kinneyyan/prompts/tree/HEAD/skills/openspec-reverse-update-spec) · ★2)

- **openspec-review**: Run the full review gate for an implemented OpenSpec change before archive. ([elevenyellow/OneJs_Boilerplate](https://github.com/elevenyellow/OneJs_Boilerplate/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/openspec-review))
- **openspec-review-change**: Review an OpenSpec change bundle for quality, consistency, and implementation readiness. Use when the user wants to analyze specs before implementation, find issues across artifacts, or assess readiness for the apply step. Similar to speckit.analyze but with iterative find-and-fix capability. Trigger for phrases like "review the spec", "check the change", "is this ready to implement", "analyze artifacts", "review before implementing". ([epam/ai-dial-admin-evaluation-framework-backend](https://github.com/epam/ai-dial-admin-evaluation-framework-backend/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/openspec-review-change) · ★4)
- **openspec-review-changes**: 現在の openspec/changes を全件横断レビューし、change 内・change 間の矛盾、重複、依存漏れ、仕様の齟齬、曖昧な判断を洗い出して、1 フェーズ最大 3 問の質問を再帰的に繰り返し、すべての疑問点を解消する。ユーザーが複数 change の整合性確認、競合確認、実装前レビュー、全 change の疑問解消、まとめて仕様を詰めることに触れたら必ずこのスキルを使う。 ([SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public](https://github.com/SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec/skills/openspec-review-changes))

- **openspec-review-pipeline**: OpenSpec の proposal から tasks まで全ドキュメントを段階的にチームレビューし、critical 指摘がなくなるまで繰り返す。レビュー済みドキュメント一式を品質保証する。 ([shiromac/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/shiromac/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/openspec-review-pipeline))
- **openspec-review-proposal**: Checklist do dev (Backend/Frontend) para revisar uma proposta OpenSpec no stage de review: confere viabilidade, tarefas faltando, arquivos/modulos reais e testes. Aprova (status done) ou pede mudancas (status in_progress + comentario no template). Nunca edita openspec/ nem escreve codigo nesta etapa. ([air-bizapps/twinforge-adobe-template](https://github.com/air-bizapps/twinforge-adobe-template/tree/HEAD/skills/openspec-review-proposal))
- **openspec-ring5-parity**: Generate and discharge Ring 5 CROSS-BACKEND PARITY evidence for a spec: one behavioural suite written against the kyo-compat carrier, executed on every published backend row, the compile-time API parity check, and the declared-divergence table for semantics that genuinely differ between ecosystems. Runs during Step 10 of /opsx:next-spec (schema v5, typesafe4s). ([gruggiero/typesafe4s](https://github.com/gruggiero/typesafe4s/tree/HEAD/openspec/schemas/verified-scala3/skills/openspec-ring5-parity) · ★4)
- **openspec-roadmap**: Decomposes a PRD or large epic into prioritized OpenSpec slices in openspec/roadmap.md and keeps slice lifecycle in sync with propose, apply, and archive. Use when a PRD exists but work is still one mega proposal, when breaking a feature into multiple OpenSpec changes, when picking the next slice, or when updating roadmap status after OpenSpec steps. ([bardaxx/skillbook](https://github.com/bardaxx/skillbook/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/openspec-roadmap) · ★3)
- **openspec-run-e2e**: Ручной сквозной (E2E) прогон уже реализованного OpenSpec-change на локальном dev-стенде — подготовка состояний в dev-БД, сценарии из «Manual checks» test-plan.md через браузер (agent-browser) и, где UI не дотягивается, через API/консоль, сверка экранов с макетами ЧТЗ из docs/ctz/, MD-отчёт со скриншотами. Используй, когда просят «проведи e2e по ченджу X», «прогони ручные проверки из test-plan.md», «проверь руками полный цикл». Это проверка поверх зелёных автотестов — тесты не пишутся, код не чинится. ([icantluvv/learn-dashboard](https://github.com/icantluvv/learn-dashboard/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/openspec-run-e2e))
